- **skill-scout**: Search local, marketplace, repository, package, GitHub, and web sources before creating a new skill or custom implementation. ([shipshitdev/skills](https://github.com/shipshitdev/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/skill-scout) · ★39)
- **skill-scout**: Vets Claude Code skills, plugins and AI tools before you install them, and finds the ones that suit your work. ([alihadidai365-ops/skill-scout](https://github.com/alihadidai365-ops/skill-scout) · ★12 · 1 skill)
- **skill-scout**: Scouts an agent conversation (current or a past saved transcript) for skill opportunities and produces a markdown report of recommended new skills, edits to existing ones, and content to simplify, then hands off to /skill-creator or edits the affected SKILL.md directly. ([donnfelker/donnfelker-plugin-marketplace](https://github.com/donnfelker/donnfelker-plugin-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/skill-scout) · ★4 · 1 skill)
- **skill-scout**: Discover, install, and run Claude Code skills from marketplaces, GitHub, and the web — all in one step. ([ez2k/skill-scout](https://github.com/ez2k/skill-scout) · 1 skill)
- **skill-scout**: Find skills that fit your project on GitHub (ranked by fit, health and stars, with their open issues), prune the ones you never use, and propose new ones from what you repeat. ([iampreetdave-max/agent-skills](https://github.com/iampreetdave-max/agent-skills) · 10 skills)

- **skill-vv**: Verification and validation for skills, across vendors. Before a skill's first real use in a session, surfaces its requirements and offers install or configuration, then checks whether the current model and platform have passed the skill's V&V procedures and offers to run them. State lives in a VV.md file with YAML frontmatter beside each skill; a Node CLI reads and records it for any agent, and a SessionStart hook runs the check automatically on hook-capable harnesses. ([gnomatix/gnomatix-oss-skills](https://github.com/gnomatix/gnomatix-oss-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/skill-vv) · 2 skills, 1 hook file)
- **skill-watch**: Claude Code + Codex hook loop for skill activation, standards drift, swarm/arch-check review signals, and reviewable auto-rewrites into this marketplace working tree. ([aaronkendell/skills-marketplace](https://github.com/aaronkendell/skills-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/skill-watch) · 3 skills, 1 hook file)
